f:T4521f,<h2 id="v6">v6<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#v6"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<h3 id="react-router-v6-정식-릴리즈">React Router v6 정식 릴리즈<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#react-router-v6-정식-릴리즈"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>2021년 11월 4일 v6.0.0 beta → release 출시되었습니다.</p>
<h3 id="react-v168-이상으로-업그레이드">React v16.8 이상으로 업그레이드<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#react-v168-이상으로-업그레이드"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>단, React ≥ 15 이상이면 react router v5 이상을 호환하고 있어서 v6로 업그레이드 하지 않더라도 react만 업그레이드 가능합니다.</p>
<ul>
<li>샘플코드: <a href="https://github.com/seungahhong/react-router-tutorial">https://github.com/seungahhong/react-router-tutorial</a></li>
</ul>
<h3 id="react-router-v6-장점">React Router v6 장점<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#react-router-v6-장점"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>기존 버전에 비해서 버들 사이즈가 70% 정도 감소했습니다.(빌드시간 감소??)</li>
</ul>
<p>v5.1 버전의 크기는 9.4kb → v6 버전의 크기는 2.9kb로 감소했습니다.</p>
<p><img src="/blog-assets/2022/03/assets/12/Untitled.png" alt="Untitled"></p>
<p><img src="/blog-assets/2022/03/assets/12/Untitled1.png" alt="Untitled"></p>
<ul>
<li>React Hooks 전반적인 지원(hoc 등 기능지원 들이 사라짐)</li>
<li>구 브라우저에 대한 지원 중단(단, polyfill 사용으로 대응가능)
<ul>
<li>IE 11 지원
<ul>
<li>package.json IE11 브라우저 지원하도록 추가</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="bash" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">"browserslist"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">    "production"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">      "..."</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">      "ie 11"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">    "development"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">      "..."</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">      "ie 11"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>polyfill 추가
<ul>
<li>react-app-polyfill(facebook 제공)
<ul>
<li>리액트 개발에서 사용하는 다양한 문법을 변환해주는 라이브러리</li>
<li>Promise, window.fetch, Symbol, Object.assign, Array.from + [ IE9 Map, Set ]와 같은 필요한 것만 포함하고 있어 사이즈가 작아 가벼운 게 특징</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// src/index.tsx</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 첫번째 라인에 추가</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-app-polyfill/ie11'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-app-polyfill/stable'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// async,await,generator 문법사용</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>상대경로 사용으로 인한 코드량 감소(match.url, match.path)</li>
<li>기능에 대한 단일화된 표준
<ul>
<li>예) Router → element 통합</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h3 id="react-router-v6-설치">React Router v6 설치<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#react-router-v6-설치"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="solidity" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="solidity" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">npm install react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">router</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">dom </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// react-router-dom@6</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="switch--routes-변경사항">Switch → Routes 변경사항<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#switch--routes-변경사항"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>Switch 처럼 순서를 기준으로 선택하는 것이 아닌, 가장 일치하는 라우트를 기반인 기능으로 변경</li>
<li>Switch → Routes 변경(route는 routes에 children이어야 함)</li>
<li>exact props 삭제
<ul>
<li>서브경로가 필요한 경우 path * 사용</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>component, children, render → element 통일</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이전</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Switch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> exact</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> component</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Home</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Not Found&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/about"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">About</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Home</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/*'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Not Found&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>

<h3 id="withrouter--hooks-변경사항">withRouter → hooks 변경사항<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#withrouter--hooks-변경사항"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>withRouter 삭제로 인한 hooks api 변경
<ul>
<li>match: useMatch → 현재 상대경로(단, 속성값이 달라져서 확인필요)</li>
<li>history: useNavigate 지원</li>
<li>location: useLocation ( v5 이후에서도 지원)</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이전</span></span>

<h3 id="usehistory--usenavigate-변경사항">useHistory → useNavigate 변경사항<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#usehistory--usenavigate-변경사항"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>useHistory hooks 대체로 useNavigate 변경</p>
<ul>
<li>useHistory 리턴값(객체) vs useNavigate 리턴값(함수)</li>
<li>기존에는 용도에 맞게 go, goBback, goForward, push, replace 메서드 호출에서 직관적인 함수호출로 변경</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이전</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">          navigation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Item/2'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">            // vs history.replace('Item/2');</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">            replace: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      ></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        Replace to Item</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Navigation.defaultProps </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> default</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> Navigation;</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<h3 id="useroutematch-변경사항">useRouteMatch 변경사항<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#useroutematch-변경사항"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>useRouteMatch 사라져서, 기존에 현재 url을 얻기위한 match.path, match.url 사라짐
<ul>
<li>현재 라우터 기준의 상대경로로 변경</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="navlink-변경사항">NavLink 변경사항<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#navlink-변경사항"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>activeStye, activeClassName 사라지고, isActive Props로 변경</p>
<ul>
<li>activeClassName → className={({ isActive }) => (isActive ? styles.active : '')}</li>
<li>activeStyle={activeStyle} → style={({ isActive }) => (isActive ? activeStyle : {})}</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p>exact → end 로 변경</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이전</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Template.defaultProps </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> default</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> Template;</span></span></code></pre></figure>

</li>

<h3 id="redirect--navigate-변경사항">Redirect → Navigate 변경사항<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#redirect--navigate-변경사항"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이전</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Switch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Redirect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/main"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> to</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/user/1"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Switch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이후</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Routes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/main"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Navigate</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> replace</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> to</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/user/1"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Routes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="renderroutes--useroutes">renderRoutes → useRoutes<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#renderroutes--useroutes"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>기존의 react-router-config의 renderRouters가 useRoutes라는 Hook 으로 변경됨.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이전</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// react-router-config</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// yarn add react-router-config로 설치 후 사용</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { renderRoutes } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react-router-config'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> routes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    component: Root,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    routes: [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        exact: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        component: Home,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/child/:id'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        component: Child,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        routes: [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Root</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Root&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/* 자식 라우트들이 렌더할 수 있도록  renderRoutes 실행 */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">renderRoutes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(route.routes)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Home</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Home&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Child</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Child&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/*  renderRoutes가 없으면 자식들은 렌더되지 않음  */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">renderRoutes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(route.routes)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> GrandChild</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">someProp</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Grand Child&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">someProp</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">ReactDOM.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">BrowserRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/* renderRoutes에 가장 처음 정의했던 routes 자체를 뿌려줌으로써 차례로 렌더링될 수 있도록 함 */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">renderRoutes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(routes)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">BrowserRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  document.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getElementById</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'root'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6 이후</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> App</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useRoutes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // Route에서 사용하는 props의 요소들과 동일</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    { path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Home</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    { path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'dashboard'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Dashboard</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'invoices'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Invoices</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // 중첩 라우트의 경우도 Route에서와 같이 children이라는 property를 사용</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      children: [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        { path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">':id'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Invoice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        { path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'sent'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">SentInvoices</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      ],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // NotFound 페이지는 다음과 같이 구현할 수 있음</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    { path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'*'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">NotFound</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // element를 return함으로써 적절한 계층으로 구성된 element가 렌더링 될 수 있도록 함</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> element;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="migrating-to-routerproviderwith-createbrowserrouter">Migrating to RouterProvider(with createBrowserRouter)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#migrating-to-routerproviderwith-createbrowserrouter"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p>도입배경</p>
<p>createBrowserRouter가 도입된 이유와 장점입니다:</p>
<ol>
<li><strong>단순화된 라우팅 설정</strong>: createBrowserRouter를 사용하면 라우트 구성을 하나의 객체로 선언할 수 있습니다. 이로 인해 라우트 설정을 보다 간단하고 일관성 있게 관리할 수 있습니다.</li>
<li><strong>라우트 데이터 로딩</strong>: createBrowserRouter는 데이터 로딩을 더 쉽게 처리할 수 있는 기능을 제공합니다. 라우트마다 데이터를 로드하고, 이를 loader 함수를 통해 처리할 수 있습니다. 이로 인해 데이터와 라우트 설정을 한 곳에서 관리할 수 있습니다.</li>
<li><strong>중첩 라우트의 간편한 관리</strong>: 중첩 라우트(nested routes)를 설정하는 것도 더 직관적입니다. createBrowserRouter는 중첩 라우트를 선언적이고 명확하게 관리할 수 있는 구조를 제공합니다.</li>
<li><strong>에러 경계(Error Boundaries)</strong>: 각 라우트에 에러 경계를 쉽게 추가할 수 있습니다. createBrowserRouter를 사용하면 특정 라우트에서 발생하는 오류를 처리하기 위해 에러 경계를 설정하는 것이 간편해집니다.</li>
<li><strong>경량화 및 최적화</strong>: React Router v6는 성능과 사용성을 고려하여 많은 부분이 최적화되었습니다. createBrowserRouter는 이러한 최신 최적화를 활용할 수 있는 구조를 제공합니다.</li>
</ol>
<h3 id="client-side-routing">client side routing<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#client-side-routing"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'react'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v5 이전</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">      path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/"</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> to</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"about"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>About Us&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      }</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Switch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">BrowserRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// v6</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// client routers</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> router</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createBrowserRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    path: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Hello World&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">h1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> to</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"about"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>About Us&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Link</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    element: &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>About&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">createRoot</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(document.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getElementById</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'root'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">render</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">RouterProvider</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> router</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">router</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> />,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="nested-routes">Nested Routes<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#nested-routes"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>path 지정 시에 상대경로를 지정해줄 수 있게 되면서 중첩 라우팅 구현이 가능하며, 자식 패스는 부모 패스의 상대 경로로 설정됩니다.</p>
<ul>
<li>createRoutesFromElements(Configure nested routes with JSX)

<h3 id="dynamicsegments">dynamicsegments<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#dynamicsegments"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"projects/:projectId/tasks/:taskId"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// If the current location is /projects/abc/tasks/3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // sent to loaders</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    params.projectId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// abc</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    params.taskId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // and actions</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    params.projectId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// abc</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    params.taskId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Task</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">/>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Task</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // returned from `useParams`</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useParams</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  params.projectId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// abc</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  params.taskId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Random</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useMatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">    "/projects/:projectId/tasks/:taskId"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  match.params.projectId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// abc</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  match.params.taskId; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<h3 id="data-loading">data loading<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#data-loading"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>navigation 동안에 데이터의 로딩을 loader를 통해서 제공하고 있습니다.</p>
<p>또한, loader를 통해서 리턴한 값을 각 element에서는 useLoaderData를 통해서 얻어 올 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// loader</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">request</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // loaders can be async functions</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> res</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fetch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/api/user.json'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      signal: request.signal,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> user</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> res.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">json</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> user;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Root</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">":teamId"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // loaders understand Fetch Responses and will automatically</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // unwrap the res.json(), so you can simply return a fetch</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fetch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`/api/teams/${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">teamId</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Team</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">      path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">":gameId"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">      loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">        // of course you can use any data store</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fakeSdk.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getTeam</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(params.gameId);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">      element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Game</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<h3 id="redirects">redirects<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#redirects"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>데이터를 로딩/변경되는 동안 라우팅을 변경할 경우 redirect 메서드를 사용해서 이동이 가능합니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"dashboard"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> user</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fake.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getUser</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">user) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // if you know you can't render the route, you can</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // throw a redirect to stop executing code here,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // sending the user to a new route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      throw</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> redirect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/login"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // otherwise continue</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> stats</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fake.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getDashboardStats</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { user, stats };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">/></span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"project/new"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">request</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> request.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">formData</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> newProject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createProject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(data);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // it's common to redirect after actions complete,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // sending the user to the new record</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> redirect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`/projects/${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">newProject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">/></span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="pending-navigation-ui">pending navigation ui<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#pending-navigation-ui"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>다음 페이지를 렌더링 하기 전에 펜딩 UI를 표기하기 위해서 navigation.state를 사용하면 됩니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Root</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> navigation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useNavigation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
