<h3 id="skeleton-ui-with-suspense">skeleton-ui-with-suspense<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#skeleton-ui-with-suspense"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>페이지 이동 시 데이터를 얻어오는 중간에 defer 메서드를 사용 시 Suspense, Await 메서드 사용해서 로딩/스켈레톤 UI 사용이 가능합니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"issue/:issueId"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Issue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // these are promises, but *not* awaited</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> comments</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fake.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getIssueComments</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(params.issueId);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> history</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fake.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getIssueHistory</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(params.issueId);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // the issue, however, *is* awaited</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> issue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> fake.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getIssue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(params.issueId);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // defer enables suspense for the un-awaited promises</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> defer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({ issue, comments, history });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">/>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Issue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">issue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">history</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">comments</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useLoaderData</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">IssueDescription</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> issue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">issue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/* Suspense provides the placeholder fallback */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Suspense</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fallback</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">IssueHistorySkeleton</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/* Await manages the deferred data (promise) */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">history</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">          {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/* this calls back when the data is resolved */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">          {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolvedHistory</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">IssueHistory</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> history</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">resolvedHistory</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Suspense</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Suspense</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fallback</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">IssueCommentsSkeleton</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">comments</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">          {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/* ... or you can use hooks to access the data */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">IssueComments</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Suspense</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<h3 id="form-data-mutations">form data mutations<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#form-data-mutations"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>form action을 사용해서 form 안에 데이터(name)를 얻어올 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Form</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/project/new"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    Target Finish Date</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">br</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"date"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"due"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"project/new"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  action</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">request</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> formData</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> request.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">formData</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> newProject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> createProject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">({</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      title: formData.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"title"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      due: formData.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"due"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    });</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> redirect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`/projects/${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">newProject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">/></span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="error-handling">error-handling<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#error-handling"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>에러가 생길 경우 errorElement 선언된 컴포넌트 렌더링이 가능합니다.</p>
<p>또한 자식 라우트에서 errorElement가 없는경우 부모 errorElement 따라가게 됩니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  loader</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    something.that.throws.an.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // this will not be rendered</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">HappyPath</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // but this will instead</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  errorElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">ErrorBoundary</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">/></span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"/"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">HappyPath</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">  errorElement</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">ErrorBoundary</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">/* Errors here bubble up to the parent route */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"login"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Login</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Route</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="optsbasename">opts.basename<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#optsbasename"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>BaseURL 설정을 해야하는 경우 createBrowserRouter 2번쨰 인자에 옵션으로 설정이 가능합니다.</p>
<p>단, createBrowserRouter 선언 컴포넌트에서 동적 param을 basename으로 설정을 해야하는 경우 window.location.pathname을 통해서 동적 param을 얻어와서 정적 baseURL을 얻어올 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">createBrowserRouter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(routes, {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  basename: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/teams'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> pathname</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> window.location.pathname;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> prefix</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> '/teams'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> pathname</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">replace</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(prefix, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">split</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">param</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> param.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ></span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (params.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ></span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      baseName: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">prefix</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}/${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">params</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">]</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      baseParam: params[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">],</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    };</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    baseParam: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>

<h3 id="prompt-재구현useblocker">Prompt 재구현(useBlocker)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#prompt-재구현useblocker"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> ImportantForm</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [value, setValue] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">useState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Block navigating elsewhere when data has been entered into the input</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> blocker </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useBlocker</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">currentLocation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">nextLocation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> currentLocation.pathname </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> nextLocation.pathname,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Form</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> method</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"post"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        Enter some important data:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">input</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">          name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"data"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">          value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">          onChange</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(e.target.value)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">label</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"submit"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Save&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">blocker.state </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'blocked'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Are you sure you want to leave?&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> onClick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> blocker.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">proceed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Proceed&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">          &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> onClick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> blocker.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">reset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>Cancel&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">button</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      ) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Form</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3 id="outlet-useoutletcontext">Outlet, useOutletContext<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#outlet-useoutletcontext"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h3>
<p>패스별로 자식 컴포넌트를 선언을 다르게 해야할 경우 location pathname 분기 후 outlet을 처리를 진행할 수 있습니다.</p>
<p>또한 자식 컴포넌트에 데이터를 넘길 경우 context props 넘기고 자식 라우터 컴포넌트는 useOutletContext에서 값을 얻어올 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  location.pathname.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">indexOf</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'/a'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Outlet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> context</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{ type: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'a'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">Outlet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> context</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{ type: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'b'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> /></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> B</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> useOutletContext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;{ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }>();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span></code></pre></figure>
